### Ran Playwright code
await page.evaluate('() => {\n  // Get all h2 elements and their text\n  const h2s = Array.from(document.querySelectorAll(\'h2\'));\n  const headings = h2s.map(h => h.textContent?.trim()).filter(Boolean);\n  \n  // Count documents under each section\n  const sections = [];\n  h2s.forEach((h2, index) => {\n    if (h2.textContent?.trim() === \'Filters\') return; // Skip filters\n    \n    // Find the next section or count docs in parent\n    const parent = h2.parentElement;\n    if (parent) {\n      const docs = parent.querySelectorAll(\'a[href^="/docs/"]\');\n      if (docs.length > 0) {\n        sections.push({\n          heading: h2.textContent?.trim(),\n          count: docs.length\n        });\n      }\n    }\n  });\n  \n  return {\n    allHeadings: headings,\n    sections: sections\n  };\n}');
